this is excel data generated from jira tickets export, even if the RCA is present it is fetching the sentences befor that also, and there are some tickets which was just a test even that are being exported, how do i handle these properly?


Looking at your Excel data, your extraction logic is getting "polluted" by two main things: greetings/polite filler (e.g., "Hi Aarthi, This Issue has been resolved...") and system/test noise (e.g., "Test Mail," "Ignore this mail").

To turn this raw dump into high-quality training data for your bot, you need to apply Rule-Based Filtering and Anchor Trimming.

1. Handling the "Greeting" Noise
Your current CleanContentByKeyword finds the keyword but keeps everything before it if it's the "longest comment." We need to enforce a stricter "Anchor" rule. If a marker like RCA: or Root Cause: is found, we should discard everything before it.

Update your CleanContentByKeyword logic:

C#
public static string CleanContentByKeyword(string rawText, string keyword)
{
    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rawText)) return string.Empty;

    string content = rawText;

    // If we have a marker, strictly start from that marker
    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(keyword))
    {
        int markerIndex = rawText.IndexOf(keyword, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        if (markerIndex != -1)
        {
            // This removes the "Hi Team, we fixed it..." part
            content = rawText.Substring(markerIndex);
        }
    }

    // List of footers to cut off the end
    string[] terminators = { "Regards", "From:", "Sent:", "Subject:", "Best Regards", "Thanks", "Thank you" };
    
    int firstTerminator = terminators
        .Select(t => content.IndexOf(t, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        .Where(idx => idx != -1)
        .DefaultIfEmpty(content.Length)
        .Min();

    return content.Substring(0, firstTerminator).Trim();
}
2. Filtering Out "Test" and "Empty" Tickets
Tickets like SWSUP-74 ("Ignore this mail") and SWSUP-2 ("Test Mail") will confuse your bot. You should add a Quality Gate before adding a ticket to your results list.

Add a filter in your FetchRcaTicketsAsync loop:

C#
// Define a list of "Noise" keywords that indicate a ticket is useless
string[] noiseKeywords = { "test mail", "ignore this", "test_ticket", "finding available", "analyzing the mentioned issue" };

bool isNoise = noiseKeywords.Any(nk => 
    issue["fields"]?["summary"]?.ToString().Contains(nk, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == true ||
    finalRca.Contains(nk, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == true
);

// Only add if it's NOT noise and the RCA has actual content
if (!isNoise && finalRca.Length > 10) 
{
    results.Add(new JiraTicket { ... });
}
3. Smart Handling of "No RCA Mentioned"
Look at SWSUP-44: It doesn't use the word "RCA," but it says "And note output was blocked due to end tag was placed inside the table."

Since you are moving toward a Semantic Kernel bot, you want to capture these "Informal RCAs."

Strategy: If no rcaMarkers are found, but the comment contains technical words like Database, Tag, Element, or Table, treat that comment as the RCA.

4. Improving the Data for Semantic Kernel
In your Excel, some RCAs are very specific (SWSUP-10 mentions Parent ID and Child ID).

To make the bot perform better: During your export, create a TechnicalTags column.

Use a simple script to scan the RCA for words like EntityDomain, PvpTableTextBlock, or File Lock.

When the bot queries the Vector DB, it will use these tags to find the exact match faster.

Comparison of Results:
Ticket	Before Cleaning	After Cleaning (Bot Ready)
SWSUP-79	Hi Aarthi, This Issue has been resolved... RCA : The system encountered a "File Lock"...	RCA : The system encountered a "File Lock" error. This occurs when... manually terminated it.
SWSUP-74	Hi Team, Ignore this mail.	(Discarded - Noise Filter)
SWSUP-10	Root Cause: The issue occurred due to the Parent ID...	Root Cause: The issue occurred due to the Parent ID and Child ID being the same in the database...
How do you want to handle tickets like SWSUP-57 where they are still "Analyzing"? Currently, these show up in your export but don't provide a solution. Should we filter those out until they are "Done" with a real RCA?




how to fetch Description in model property?

To fetch the Description field from the Jira API and map it to your model, you need to target fields["description"].

However, since you are using API v3, the description is returned in Atlassian Document Format (ADF)—the same JSON structure used for comments. You must pass it through your DataCleaner.ConvertAdfToText method to get readable string content.

1. Update the JiraTicket Model
Add the Description property to your class.

C#
public class JiraTicket
{
    public string? Key { get; set; }
    public string? Summary { get; set; }
    public string? Priority { get; set; }
    public string? Description { get; set; } // New property
    public string? RcaComment { get; set; }
    public string? Workaround { get; set; }
}
2. Update the FetchRcaTicketsAsync Logic
You need to do two things:

Ensure the description field is requested in the URL.

Extract and clean the description inside the loop.

Step A: Update the URL
Add description to the fields parameter in your query string.

C#
string url =
    $"{jira["BaseUrl"]}/rest/api/3/search/jql" +
    $"?jql={Uri.EscapeDataString(jql)}" +
    $"&fields=summary,priority,comment,description" + // Added description here
    $"&maxResults=100";
Step B: Map the value in the loop
Inside your foreach (var issue in json["issues"]) loop, extract the description using your existing utility.

C#
foreach (var issue in json["issues"])
{
    // 1. Get the raw ADF description and convert to text
    var rawDescriptionAdf = issue["fields"]?["description"];
    var cleanDescription = DataCleaner.ConvertAdfToText(rawDescriptionAdf);

    // ... (your existing RCA/Workaround logic) ...

    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(finalRca))
    {
        results.Add(new JiraTicket
        {
            Key = issue["key"]?.ToString(),
            Summary = issue["fields"]?["summary"]?.ToString(),
            Priority = issue["fields"]?["priority"]?["name"]?.ToString(),
            Description = cleanDescription, // Mapping the cleaned description
            RcaComment = finalRca,
            Workaround = finalWorkaround ?? "No manual workaround available"
        });
    }
}
3. Why this is useful for your Bot
By fetching the description into the model, you can now perform the Diagnostic Mapping we discussed earlier. You can run regex against ticket.Description to find the Workspace ID or Rainbow URL automatically.

4. Excel Update
Don't forget to add a column in your ExcelService to actually see this data in your export:

C#
ws.Cell(1, 5).Value = "Description";
// ... in the loop ...
ws.Cell(row, 5).Value = t.Description;
Note on "Test" tickets: Now that you have the description, you can improve your filter even more. If the Description is empty or just says "test," you can automatically skip that ticket to keep your Vector DB clean.

System.NullReferenceException

  HResult=0x80004003

  Message=Object reference not set to an instance of an object.

  Source=XbrlSupportBot

  StackTrace:

   at XbrlSupportBot.Services.JiraService.<>c__DisplayClass3_2.<FetchRcaTicketsAsync>b__5(String nk) in D:\MELBIN JOE\JOE\DotNet\Core\XbrlSupportBot\Services\JiraService.cs:line 106





in line ->

                                     issue["fields"]?["summary"]?.ToString().Contains(nk, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == true ||

                                     finalRca.Contains(nk, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == true);





full code ->



using ClosedXML.Excel;

using DocumentFormat.OpenXml.Spreadsheet;

using Newtonsoft.Json.Linq;

using System;

using System.Net.Http.Headers;

using System.Text;

using XbrlSupportBot.Models;

using XbrlSupportBot.Utilities;



namespace XbrlSupportBot.Services

{



    public class JiraService : IJiraService

    {

        private readonly IConfiguration _config;

        private readonly HttpClient _client;



        public JiraService(IConfiguration config, HttpClient client)

        {

            _config = config;

            _client = client;

        }



        public async Task<List<JiraTicket>> FetchRcaTicketsAsync()

        {

            var jira = _config.GetSection("Jira");



            var auth = Convert.ToBase64String(

                Encoding.ASCII.GetBytes($"{jira["Email"]}:{jira["ApiToken"]}")

            );



            _client.DefaultRequestHeaders.Authorization =

                new AuthenticationHeaderValue("Basic", auth);





            _client.DefaultRequestHeaders.Accept.Add(

                    new MediaTypeWithQualityHeaderValue("application/json"));





            string jql = $"project = '{jira["ProjectKey"]}' AND status = 'Done'";

            //string url = $"{jira["BaseUrl"]}/rest/api/2/search?jql={Uri.EscapeDataString(jql)}&fields=summary,priority,comment&maxResults=100";







            string url =

                $"{jira["BaseUrl"]}/rest/api/3/search/jql" +

                $"?jql={Uri.EscapeDataString(jql)}" +

                $"&fields=description,summary,priority,comment" +

                $"&maxResults=100";



            //url = "https://datatracks.atlassian.net/rest/api/2/search/jql?jql=project%20%3D%20%27SWSUP%27%20AND%20status%20%3D%20%27Done%27&fields=summary,priority,comment&maxResults=100";



            var response = await _client.GetStringAsync(url);

            var json = JObject.Parse(response);





            //if (!response.IsSuccessStatusCode)

            //{

            //    throw new Exception($"Jira error {response.StatusCode}: {content}");

            //}



            // Define keywords that signal useful knowledge

            string[] rcaMarkers = { "RCA", "Root Cause", "Resolution", "Fix", "Solution" };



            var results = new List<JiraTicket>();



            foreach (var issue in json["issues"])

            {

               // Get the raw ADF description and convert to text

                var rawDescription = issue["fields"]?["description"];

                var cleanDescription = DataCleaner.ConvertAdfToText(rawDescription);



                var rawComments = issue["fields"]?["comment"]?["comments"]?

                      .Select(c => DataCleaner.ConvertAdfToText(c["body"]))

                      .ToList();



                string rcaRaw = null;

                string detectedMarker = "";



               

                var workaroundRaw = rawComments?.FirstOrDefault(c => c.Contains("Workaround:", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));

                

                //Look specifically for comments containing markers in the WHOLE list first

                var commentWithMarker = rawComments?.FirstOrDefault(c =>

                    rcaMarkers.Any(m => c.Contains(m, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)));



                if (commentWithMarker != null)

                {

                    rcaRaw = commentWithMarker;

                    // Identify which marker was used so we can clean it correctly

                    detectedMarker = rcaMarkers.FirstOrDefault(m =>

                        rcaRaw.Contains(m, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));

                }

                else if (rawComments?.Any() == true) // ONLY if no marker is found in ANY comment, fall back to the longest one

                {

                    rcaRaw = rawComments.OrderByDescending(c => c.Length).First();

                }



                // Clean using the detected marker (or empty string if we fell back to length)

                var finalRca = DataCleaner.CleanContentByKeyword(rcaRaw, detectedMarker);

                var finalWorkaround = DataCleaner.CleanContentByKeyword(workaroundRaw, "Workaround:");



                // Define a list of "Noise" keywords that indicate a ticket is useless

                string[] noiseKeywords = { "test mail", "ignore this", "test_ticket", "finding available", "analyzing the mentioned issue" };

                bool isNoise = noiseKeywords.Any(nk =>

                                                    issue["fields"]?["summary"]?.ToString().Contains(nk, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == true ||

                                                    finalRca.Contains(nk, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == true);



                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(finalRca) && !isNoise && finalRca.Length > 10)

                {

                    results.Add(new JiraTicket

                    {

                        Key = issue["key"]?.ToString(),

                        Description = cleanDescription,

                        Summary = issue["fields"]?["summary"]?.ToString(),

                        Priority = issue["fields"]?["priority"]?["name"]?.ToString(),

                        RcaComment = finalRca ?? "No specific RCA documented",

                        Workaround = finalWorkaround ?? "No manual workaround available"

                    });

                }

            }



            return results;

        }

    }



}

The NullReferenceException is occurring because finalRca is likely null.

In your code, if an issue has no comments at all, rcaRaw stays null. When you pass null into DataCleaner.CleanContentByKeyword, it returns null (or a null-valued string) into finalRca. Then, when you call finalRca.Contains(...), the application crashes because you can't run a "Contains" check on something that doesn't exist.

The Fix
You need to add a null-check (using the ? operator) to finalRca inside the isNoise logic.

Update your noise filter logic to this:

C#
bool isNoise = noiseKeywords.Any(nk =>
    (issue["fields"]?["summary"]?.ToString()?.Contains(nk, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == true) ||
    (finalRca?.Contains(nk, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == true) // Added '?' after finalRca
);
Why this happened
In C#, null.Contains("anything") results in an immediate crash. By adding the ?, you are saying: "If finalRca is null, don't even try to check for noise, just treat the whole expression as null/false."

Pro-Tip for cleaner code
To make your foreach loop more robust and avoid these "Object reference not set" errors in the future, you can ensure finalRca is never null by using the Null Coalescing Operator (??):

C#
// Change this line:
var finalRca = DataCleaner.CleanContentByKeyword(rcaRaw, detectedMarker);

// To this (Ensures it is at least an empty string, never null):
var finalRca = DataCleaner.CleanContentByKeyword(rcaRaw, detectedMarker) ?? string.Empty;
Summary of what caused the crash:
A Jira ticket had no comments.

rcaRaw was null.

finalRca became null.

finalRca.Contains(...) threw the NullReferenceException.

Applying the ? or ?? as shown above will get your bot running again and allow it to skip those empty tickets safely!

To improve your bot's performance, we can extract the specific Product, Environment, Entity, and Workspace ID directly from these long descriptions. This allows the bot to perform a "Lookup" against your mapping table before it even starts searching the Vector DB.

1. The Strategy: Structured Extraction
Since your descriptions are essentially email chains, they follow predictable patterns. We can use Regex (Regular Expressions) to pull the "Context" out of the noise.

The Logic Flow:

URL Extraction: Find the IP/Port (e.g., 10.9.9.68:1800).

Environment Mapping: Compare that URL against your provided list to identify if it's "Rainbow Live" or "ESEF UAT".

Entity/WS Identification: Pull the specific job details to allow the bot to query the live database for that specific workspace.

2. Implementation: Enhanced Data Extraction
Add these methods to your DataCleaner utility to turn the messy text you provided into structured data.

C#
public static (string Entity, string WsId, string Url) ExtractJobDetails(string description)
{
    // Regex patterns for your specific email formats
    var entityMatch = Regex.Match(description, @"Entity:\s*([^\r\n*]+)", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
    var wsIdMatch = Regex.Match(description, @"WS ID:\s*([^\r\n*]+)", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
    var urlMatch = Regex.Match(description, @"http://10\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+:\d+", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);

    return (
        entityMatch.Success ? entityMatch.Groups[1].Value.Trim() : "Unknown",
        wsIdMatch.Success ? wsIdMatch.Groups[1].Value.Trim() : "Unknown",
        urlMatch.Success ? urlMatch.Value.Trim() : "Unknown"
    );
}
3. Integrating with your JiraService
In your main loop, call this extraction method. This allows you to tag the ticket with the correct Database and Product based on the tables you shared earlier.

C#
foreach (var issue in json["issues"])
{
    var rawDescription = DataCleaner.ConvertAdfToText(issue["fields"]?["description"]);
    var details = DataCleaner.ExtractJobDetails(rawDescription);
    
    // logic to map details.Url to DB Name (e.g., if Url contains :1800, Db = "Rainbow3_Prod_2")
    string dbContext = MapUrlToDb(details.Url); 

    results.Add(new JiraTicket
    {
        Key = issue["key"]?.ToString(),
        Description = rawDescription,
        EntityName = details.Entity,
        WorkspaceId = details.WsId,
        DatabaseName = dbContext,
        // ... rest of mapping
    });
}
4. Why this makes the Bot better
By using the Description, the bot now understands the Hierarchy of the Issue.
hierarchical data structure, AI generated
Getty Images
Explore

Feature	Without Description Extraction	With Description Extraction
Search Accuracy	Searches for "Output Issue" (Too broad).	Searches for "Output Issue" + "Rainbow3_Prod_2".
Live Diagnostics	Bot can only suggest past fixes.	Bot can use the WorkspaceId to check the EntityDomain table in the live DB.
Automatic Routing	Human must tell the bot which server.	Bot sees 10.9.9.68:1800 and knows it is the Rainbow Live environment.
5. Cleaning the "Noise"
In the descriptions you provided, there is a lot of "Created via e-mail received from..." or disclaimer text.

Refined Cleaning Rule:
When saving to the Vector DB, truncate the description after the first occurrence of "Regards," or "This email and any attachments...". This prevents the AI from getting confused by the names of the people in the email thread and keeps the focus on the Technical Problem.
